# MASCI Operational Execution Role and Ownership Matrix

## 1. Role Authority

This matrix provides exhaustive role/object/action permission governance. Every required role/object/action combination resolves to exactly one permitted value.

## 2. Permitted Values

- ALLOWED
- READ_ONLY
- PROPOSE_ONLY
- APPROVAL_REQUIRED
- SYSTEM_DERIVED
- PROHIBITED
- NOT_APPLICABLE

## 3. Governed Roles

- Platform Admin
- Company Admin
- Owner/President
- Executive
- Operations Leadership
- PM
- Co-PM
- Project Engineer
- Superintendent
- Foreman
- Field Leadership
- Dispatch
- Transportation
- Driver
- Shop
- Equipment/Fleet
- Safety
- HR
- QA/QC
- Survey
- Accounting
- Qualifications/Training Manager
- Read-Only Leadership
- System Automation
- AI Assistant

## 4. Governed Actions

- View
- Create
- Edit
- Assign
- Reassign
- Submit
- Review
- Approve
- Commit
- Publish
- Release
- Start
- Pause
- Block
- Unblock
- Resolve
- Complete
- Verify
- Reconcile
- Revise
- Supersede
- Cancel
- Close
- Reopen
- Export
- Administer
- Receive Notification
- Receive Brief
- Certify
- Provide Field Acceptance
- Provide Executive Acceptance

- Organization/Tenant

## 6. Canonical Owner and Separation-of-Duties Matrix

| Object | Canonical Owner | Operational Contributors | Read Consumers | Approval Owner | Escalation Owner | Backup Owner | SLA / Response Expectation | Separation-of-Duties Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Organization/Tenant | Company Admin / Platform authority | Company Admin | All scoped roles | Owner/President | Owner/President | Platform Admin | governance-critical | Segregate platform administration from tenant business-source editing |
| Person/Professional Identity | Identity authority | HR | All scoped domains | HR | HR | Company Admin | identity-critical | Identity edits require HR or governed admin lane |
| Employee/Company Membership | HR | Qualifications/Training Manager | PM, Safety, Dispatch, Operations | HR | HR | Company Admin | workforce-critical | Qualification approvers may not self-certify |
| Project | jobs_master | PM / Operations Leadership | All scoped domains | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Company Admin | operational-critical | Project identity edits separated from downstream schedule publication |
| Project Team Assignment | project_team_assignments | PM / Company Admin | Scoped staffing consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Company Admin | staffing-critical | Assignment approver separate from field actual capture |
| Company Cost-Code Catalog Item | Company cost-code authority | Company Admin / Accounting | Scoped operations consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Accounting | classification-critical | Catalog approver separate from project usage where feasible |
| Project Cost Code | Project cost-code authority | PM | Work, schedule, report consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Company Admin | project-coding-critical | Project mapping approver separate from field capture |
| Cost-Code Alias | Cost-code alias authority | PM / Company Admin | Mapping/search/history consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Company Admin | compatibility-critical | Alias creation never changes canonical code identity |
| Unit | Unit governance authority | Company Admin / Accounting | KPI and production consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Company Admin | unit-critical | Unit approver separate from affected KPI publication |
| Work Area | Work-area authority | Survey / PM | Work, schedule, report consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Project Engineer | field-critical | Merge/split approval separate from field actual submission |
| Operational Work | Operational Work authority | PM / Superintendent / Field Leadership | Schedule, reconciliation, brief consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | PM | core-work-critical | Commit/publish separated from field proposal roles |
| Schedule Activity | Schedule authority | PM / Superintendent / Field Leadership | Schedule/reconciliation/brief consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Co-PM | commitment-critical | Publication approval separate from proposal roles |
| Schedule Window | Schedule authority | PM / Operations Leadership | Schedule consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Co-PM | window-critical | Window publication separated from field proposal roles |
| Published Schedule | Schedule authority | PM / Operations Leadership | Execution/reconciliation/brief consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Company Admin | publication-critical | History cannot be silently replaced |
| Schedule Revision | Schedule authority | PM / Operations Leadership | Schedule consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Company Admin | version-critical | Revision approval separated from proposal where feasible |
| Resource Demand | Source domain owner | PM / Superintendent / Dispatch / Shop | Readiness/schedule consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Source domain backup owner | planning-critical | Demand declaration separate from approval where material |
| Resource Conflict | Conflict evaluation authority | Ops / PM / Dispatch / Shop | Readiness/schedule consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Source domain owner | action-critical | Conflict resolution visible to affected owners |
| Constraint | Constraint authority | Source domain + PM | Schedule/readiness/reconciliation/brief consumers | Operations Leadership or Safety | Operations Leadership or Safety | Source domain backup owner | blocker-critical | Hold owners separated from production fact owners |
| Readiness Evaluation | Readiness authority | PM / Dispatch / Shop / Safety / QA/QC / HR | Schedule/executive consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | PM | go/no-go critical | Readiness approver cannot fabricate source facts |
| Daily Report | daily_reports | Foreman / Superintendent / Field Leadership | PM, HR, Safety, ODS, reconciliation | PM workflow lane only | PM workflow lane only | Superintendent | field-actual-critical | PM approves workflow where governed but may not rewrite submitted actuals |
| Daily Report Work Item | Daily Report authority | Foreman / Superintendent / Field Leadership | ODS, work linkage, reconciliation | PM workflow lane only | PM workflow lane only | Superintendent | item-actual-critical | Item amendments preserve field authorship |
| Production Actual | Daily Report authority / governed actual projection | Field capture lanes | ODS, KPI, reconciliation | PM / Operations Leadership | PM / Operations Leadership | ODS projection authority | actuals-critical | Planning roles cannot rewrite source actuals |
| Labor Actual | Daily Report authority / HR qualifier authority | Field capture lanes / HR | Payroll/KPI/reconciliation consumers | HR | HR | PM | labor-critical | HR qualification does not rewrite field time truth |
| Equipment Actual | Daily Report authority / Equipment authority by concept | Field capture lanes / Equipment-Fleet | KPI/reconciliation consumers | Equipment/Fleet | Equipment/Fleet | Shop | asset-use critical | Shop cannot rewrite work completion through equipment state |
| Material Actual | Daily Report authority / source material domain | Field capture lanes / PM | KPI/reconciliation/brief consumers | PM | PM | Operations Leadership | material-critical | Material fact owners separate from brief publication |
| Trucking Actual | Transportation/Dispatch/Daily Report linkage authority | Dispatch / Transportation | KPI/reconciliation/brief consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Dispatch | transport-critical | Dispatch owns movement truth, not work completion truth |
| ODS Fact/Projection | ODS authority | System Automation | Dashboards/brief/analytics consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Platform Admin | derived-critical | ODS may not mutate source truth |
| Weekly Reconciliation | Reconciliation authority | PM / Operations Leadership | Brief/dashboard consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | PM | close-loop-critical | Reconciliation publisher cannot erase prior plan/actual lineage |
| Carry-Forward | Schedule/Reconciliation authority | PM / Operations Leadership | Schedule/reconciliation consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | PM | lineage-critical | Carry-forward preserves predecessor linkage |
| Executive Attention Item | Executive attention authority | Executive / Operations Leadership | Executive/PM/Safety/HR consumers | Owner/President | Owner/President | Executive | intervention-critical | Dismissal requires reason and retained history |
| KPI Definition | KPI owner per contract | Source domain owners | Dashboards/brief consumers | Operations Leadership | Operations Leadership | Company Admin | decision-critical | Formula approval separate from dashboard rendering |
| Dashboard Projection | Dashboard authority | System Automation / source domains | Role-scoped users | Owning role lane | Owning role lane | Source domain owner | projection-critical | Projections cannot become source truth |
| Daily Company Operations Brief | Brief authority / Operational Intelligence | Executive / Operations reviewers | Leadership consumers | Owner/President / Executive | Owner/President / Executive | Operations Leadership | brief-critical | Publication approval separate from generation |
| Brief Revision | Brief authority | Executive / Operations reviewers | Leadership consumers | Executive | Executive | Operations Leadership | brief-version-critical | Revision cannot silently replace prior version |
| Notification | Notification authority over existing ecosystem | Source workflow owner | Target recipients | Operations Leadership / domain owner | Operations Leadership / domain owner | Company Admin | delivery-critical | Notification config separate from delivery and source truth |
| Evidence/Attachment Reference | Source-record authority | Source workflow roles | Scoped viewers | Source domain escalation owner | Source domain escalation owner | Company Admin | evidence-critical | Access inherits parent object authority |
| Audit Record | Audit authority | System Automation | Scoped auditors/admins | Platform Admin | Platform Admin | Company Admin | audit-critical | Append-only, no business-source editing |
| Trust Event | Trust Spine authority | System Automation | Certification/operations consumers | Platform Admin | Platform Admin | Company Admin | trust-critical | Append-only, no business-source editing |
| Domain Event | Event authority | System Automation / source workflow emitters | Scoped consumers | Platform Admin / domain owner | Platform Admin / domain owner | Company Admin | event-critical | Events evidence transitions, never replace source records |
| Certification Artifact | Certification authority | Engineers/operators/executives by lane | Approvers/auditors | Operations Leadership / Executive / Jaymn milestone lane | Operations Leadership / Executive / Jaymn milestone lane | Platform Admin | release-critical | Certification lanes separated by role and acceptance type |

## 8. Permanent Boundary Rules

- AI Assistant is PROHIBITED from approving, committing, publishing, reconciling, certifying, closing, reopening, clearing holds, or owning canonical records.
- System Automation may perform SYSTEM_DERIVED actions only where deterministic rules are constitutionally authorized.
- Platform Admin and Company Admin do not automatically receive business-source editing authority merely because they administer the platform.
- Executives cannot rewrite Daily Report actuals.
- PMs cannot rewrite submitted field actuals.
- Foremen cannot publish company or project schedule commitments unless the explicit matrix permits a delegated, approval-required workflow.
- Dispatch owns assignments and movement execution, not production completion.
- Shop owns repair and recovery states, not schedule commitments or work completion.
- Safety owns safety holds and controls, not production facts.
- HR owns workforce and qualification truth, not operational completion.
- QA/QC owns inspection and testing truth, not Daily Report production truth.
- Accounting receives governed operational/financial mappings but does not own field work truth.

## 9. Manual GitHub and Deployment Boundary

ONLY JAYMN MAY PHYSICALLY SAVE OR PUBLISH CHANGES TO GITHUB.
ONLY JAYMN MAY PHYSICALLY DEPLOY PREVIEW OR PRODUCTION.
Emergent must never claim those manual actions occurred.

## 10. Normative Cross-References

- See the Constitution for doctrine and non-negotiable boundaries.
- See the Appendix for stable identifiers, lifecycle catalogs, event contracts, KPI/dashboard contracts, notification contracts, brief contract, security contract, and manual deployment milestones.
- See the Zero-Drift Matrix for foundational object, security, and duplication boundaries.
- See the Requirement Traceability Matrix for requirement-level mappings.
